/IndexedDB/idbcursor_advance_index.any.html OK 8 / 8

8 passing subtests
  1. index - iterate cursor number of times specified by count
  2. attempt to pass a count parameter that is not a number
  3. index - attempt to advance backwards
  4. index - iterate to the next record
  5. Calling advance() with count argument 0 should throw TypeError.
  6. Calling advance() should throws an exception TransactionInactiveError when the transaction is not active.
  7. Calling advance() should throw DOMException when the cursor is currently being iterated.
  8. If the cursor's source or effective object store has been deleted, the implementation MUST throw a DOMException of type InvalidStateError
